# Review checklist: James Naismith (naismith)

Generated 2026-11-02 by tools/export.py (preview export).

This is a preview. Every block of wordpress.html is stamped "DRAFT — NOT REVIEWED"; do not publish it.

Facts: disputed 14, rejected 6, single-source 73, verified 39. Media: disputed 41, rejected 7, verified 29.

How to use this checklist: for each item, open the source link and find the quote, then tick one box (for an edit, write the new wording). Return the file to the maintainer, who records the decisions in review/decisions.jsonl and runs tools/apply_review.py. Approval does not override the checks: only verified items can be approved and published. Disputed, conflicting and single-source items can be edited (then checked again), rejected, or given a note.

## 8. Draft content (12 units)

Each line is one unit of drafts/profile.json or of the experience chapters (content:...) and the facts it cites. Included units are in wordpress.html; withheld ones are not, for the reason given. The final export also needs, for each sentence, title and caption, a current "supported" verdict from the sentence adversary (drafts/grounding.jsonl, G6).

- [ ] profile:summary:0: James Naismith was born in Almonte, Ontario on November 6, 1861. (facts NAI-F-0035; included)
- [ ] profile:summary:1: James Naismith invented the game of basketball in 1891. (facts NAI-F-0067; included)
- [ ] profile:summary:2: He coached basketball at the University of Kansas. (facts NAI-F-0051; included)
- [ ] profile:sections:basketball:0: The assignment was to create a game that could be played indoors between the football and baseball seasons. (facts NAI-F-0078; included)
- [ ] profile:sections:basketball:1: The first game of basketball was played in 1891. (facts NAI-F-0079; included)
- [ ] profile:sections:basketball:title: The first game of basketball (facts NAI-F-0078, NAI-F-0079; included)
- [ ] profile:sections:author:0: James Naismith is the author of Basket ball, with Luther Halsey Gulick as joint author. (facts NAI-F-0071; included)
- [ ] profile:sections:author:1: Basket ball was published in New York City by American sports publishing company, c1894. (facts NAI-F-0072; included)
- [ ] profile:sections:author:title: Author of Basket ball (facts NAI-F-0071, NAI-F-0072; included)
- [ ] profile:timeline:0: James Naismith was born in Almonte, Ontario on November 6, 1861. (facts NAI-F-0035; included)
- [ ] profile:timeline:1: James Naismith invented the game of basketball in 1891. (facts NAI-F-0067; included)
- [ ] profile:artifacts:0: Basket ball by James Naismith, with Luther Halsey Gulick as joint author, was published in New York City by American sports publishing company, c1894. (facts NAI-F-0071, NAI-F-0072; included)

## 10. How to cite

Generated from inductee.json for the page; it states no fact about the inductee. A how_to_cite text in drafts/profile.json is not published.

James Naismith. Immigrant Hall of Honor. https://immigranthallofhonor.org/inductees/inductee-james-naismith/

## 11. Gaps (14)

Parts of the life story with no verified source yet. These are not errors.

- Family (parents, siblings, upbringing, marriages, children): no verified fact yet; 13 candidates deferred in staging, several conflicting with each other on the year he was orphaned and the day of his first marriage.
- Education (McGill, Presbyterian College, YMCA Training School, Gross Medical College): no verified fact yet; 22 candidates deferred, with conflicts on the year and subject of the McGill degree; IHH page degree claims (physical education degree 1888, M.D. 1898) not independently confirmed.
- Arrival in the United States: no verified source yet; NAI-F-0001 (IHH page) is single-source and gives a settlement place, not an entry date, port or mode; NAI-F-0006 is disputed (compound claim); NAI-F-0007, NAI-F-0039 and NAI-F-0040 are single-source.
- Citizenship: no verified fact yet; NAI-F-0008, NAI-F-0064 and NAI-F-0065 are disputed (compound claim or live catalogue record unreadable) and NAI-F-0041 is single-source.
- Legacy: no verified fact yet; NAI-F-0069 is disputed and legacy candidates (hall of fame naming, stamps, Olympic appearance, rules document, National Historic Person designation, audio recording) were deferred over the fact cap.
- Career: Denver YMCA (NAI-F-0049), McGill athletics (NAI-F-0020), Kansas National Guard and Mexican frontier service (NAI-F-0025, NAI-F-0057), ordination (NAI-F-0058) and France 1917-1919 (NAI-F-0026, NAI-F-0059) are single-source; retirement date is disputed (NAI-F-0056) and single-source (NAI-F-0023, NAI-F-0024).
- Awards: FIBA Hall of Fame (NAI-F-0028), Canada's Sports Hall of Fame (NAI-F-0029), Ottawa Sport Hall of Fame (NAI-F-0030), McGill Sports Hall of Fame (NAI-F-0031), McGill 1938 degree (NAI-F-0033), FIBA honorary president (NAI-F-0063) and the honorary MPE (NAI-F-0062) are single-source; the honorary degree year is disputed (NAI-F-0032, NAI-F-0077).
- Invention details: date of the first game (NAI-F-0015), December 1891 dating (NAI-F-0009, NAI-F-0013), ten-foot height (NAI-F-0011), team size (NAI-F-0068) and influences (NAI-F-0016) are single-source.
- Death: place of burial (NAI-F-0004), home address (NAI-F-0002) and cause of death (NAI-F-0038) are single-source; NAI-F-0076 is disputed (live catalogue record unreadable).
- IHH page claims not independently confirmed: NAI-F-0001 (settlement 1890); deferred IHH claims on a football helmet (1893), Head of Physical Education at the Training School (1891), Kansas department chairmanship, M.D. 1898 and a 1991 Canadian stamp.
- Single-fact sections: the summary and the author section rest on one fact each per sentence; the McGill and Springfield section has two facts and none on his studies there.
- Media: 18 of 32 items disputed and 7 rejected (no rights statement); NAI-M-0001 and NAI-M-0002 carry no confirmed licence (rights not evaluated or unknown); most Springfield College digital collection items need live confirmation of the catalogue record.
- Search families: identify produced nothing (discovery only); NARA produced one item from 33 searches; 71 of 75 corroboration searches added no new source.
- Quotes: no verified quote in Naismith's own words; candidates from the 1939 radio interview transcript and secondary quotations were deferred.

## 12. Rejected (13)

Kept in the log for the record; no action needed.

- NAI-F-0024: James Naismith remained at the University of Kansas from 1898 until his retirement in 1938.
- NAI-F-0106: Dr. Naismith, the new physical director, brought basket ball to Kansas university people in 1898.
- NAI-F-0107: The KU men's basketball team practiced at Robinson Gymnasium in 1907, the year KU's first freestanding gym opened.
- NAI-F-0113: At the 1936 Olympics in Berlin Naismith addressed all of the international teams and served as honorary referee.
- NAI-F-0119: Naismith was orphaned in 1873.
- NAI-F-0132: In 1961 a 4-cent Basketball stamp, the Naismith - Basketball Issue, honored Dr. James Naismith, the inventor of the game.
